This ledger records research attempts so selection bias remains visible.
It is generated from research/lab.sqlite; the database is the source of
truth. Do not hand-edit the table below, edit the database through
lab/registry.py instead.

| H-005 | 2026-10-30T13:49:38.324198+00:00 | VolatilitySqueezeBreakout | Closed 1h candles only; signals evaluated at bar close, order executed at next bar open. Long only, one position, whole balance. Definitions (all use completed bars): R(t) = (highest high of the 168 bars ending at t - lowest low of the 168 bars ending at t) / close(t). Squeeze(t) is true if R(t) is <= the 20th percentile of R over the 2160 bars ending at t-1 (rolling quantile 0.20, excluding the current bar). ENTRY at close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) Squeeze was true on at least one of the previous 24 bars (t-24..t-1); (2) close(t) > highest high of the prior 168 bars (t-168..t-1, excluding current bar); (3) close(t) > SMA(2400) of close; (4) no open position; (5) cooldown satisfied. EXIT at close of bar t when close(t) < lowest low of the prior 336 bars (t-336..t-1, excluding current), or via the hard stop. STOP-LOSS: fixed 8% below entry price (native intrabar stop). COOLDOWN: no re-entry for 72 candles after any exit. No take-profit, no pyramiding, no other filters. Warm-up: 2400 bars. | No price data viewed; only the context file and validation protocol (BTC, ETH, BNB 1h description through 2026-10-30). | 3 | rejected at exploration [code archived] | Long breakout after a multi-day volatility squeeze in an established uptrend |

ENTRY at close of bar t when REGIME holds AND min(close over bars t-48..t-1) <= 0.95 * max(high over bars t-336..t-1) (a pullback of at least 5% from the 14-day high occurred within the last 48 bars) AND close(t) > SMA(48) of close AND close(t-1) <= SMA(48) at t-1 (fresh recovery cross above the 2-day mean) AND no open position AND cooldown satisfied. STOP-LOSS: fixed 5% below entry price (native intrabar stop). COOLDOWN: no re-entry for 48 candles after any exit. No pyramiding, no other filters. | H-011 | 2026-11-03T14:11:52.700407+00:00 | TripleBottomReversal | Closed candles only; signal at candle close, order at next open. Long only, one position, no averaging down. Pivot low at i = lowest low of [i-5,i+5] (ties allowed on the left only), KNOWN only at the close of candle i+5. Three consecutive pivot lows L1,L2,L3 (i1<i2<i3) form the support zone if (max L - min L)/mean L <= 2%, consecutive bottoms >= 12 candles apart, i3-i1 <= 150 candles. Recoveries: H1 = highest high in (i1,i2), H2 = highest high in (i2,i3); both must be >= mean bottom * 1.03. Neckline = max(H1,H2). ENTRY at the first close above the neckline at t in [i3+5, i3+5+30] and before a fourth pivot is confirmed, if no earlier close since i3 was above it, no low since i3 is below min bottom * 0.98, volume(t) >= mean volume of the previous 20 candles. Optional filters OFF in the tested rule: RSI14 higher at i3 than at i1 (positive divergence), close > EMA200. STOP at min bottom - 0.5*ATR14(t) (fixed at entry, via custom_stoploss from the signal candle). TARGET neckline + 1.0*(neckline - mean bottom). TIME STOP 150 candles. Min R/R filter configurable, OFF (0.0) because stop below support and entry at the neckline make R/R < 1 by construction. A pattern signals once and a later pattern must start after the previous signalled pattern's last bottom (no duplicate entries). Warm-up: 800 bars. EXPOSURE: fixed share of the account chosen by the lab rule (largest 5% grid value with block-bootstrap p95 closed-trade drawdown <= 25% on all pairs, computed from this rule's own exploration trades at base cost and full exposure; calibration run; fewer than 30 trades on a pair drops the hypothesis, no retuning). Validation and final test not read. Parameters are textbook values fixed before any result. | BTC, ETH, BNB 1h and 4h candles through 2026-10-30 (4h downloaded 2026-11-03); results of H-001..H-010 known, including the one-time validation read of H-008 (a different rule on the same 2024-07..2026-10 window) - the validation split is therefore not blind for this family of ideas. | H-015 | 2026-11-04T00:11:34.975802+00:00 | TimeSeriesMomentum | Closed DAILY candles (Freqtrade informative 1d merged with merge_informative_pair), executed on 1h at the next open. Long only, one position, whole allowed balance, no stop-loss, no sizing hooks. mom(d) = close(d)/close(d-N days) - 1. ENTRY while mom > 0 (and, with the regime, regime_on() true = daily close > SMA(200); vol_regime off per the step-1.4 ablation). EXIT when mom <= 0 (or the regime turns off). GRID (pre-declared, complete): N in {30, 60, 90, 120, 180} x regime in {off, on} = 10 configurations; N_trials for the DSR = 10. Each configuration is also a test at costs base and base_2x on each of BTC, ETH, BNB. Warm-up 400 bars (daily and 1h). PASS CRITERIA TO READ VALIDATION (fixed before running, all required): DSR of the best configuration (N=10) >= 0.95; PBO (CSCV, 16 slices) <= 0.5; plateau (neighbouring N with the same regime flag reach >= 70% of the best Sharpe); Sharpe above buy-and-hold AND above Sma200Baseline at base_2x in the IS; >= 30 trades per pair; lab/config/gates.json clear on exploration. Fail = family rejected and archived. The validation split is then read ONCE, for the configuration chosen in the IS. Final test / prospective holdout not read. | BTC, ETH, BNB 1h/4h/1d candles through 2026-11-02; results of H-001..H-014 known, including the one-time validation read of H-008 and the Sma200Baseline cycle table (so the validation window is not blind for trend-following ideas). | H-018 | 2026-11-04T18:26:46.153196+00:00 | DonchianEnsemble | Protocol docs/protocolo-validacao-v3.md section 2.2. Signals come from CLOSED daily candles (merge_informative_pair, ffill, as in Sma200Baseline) and are executed on the 1h chart at the next open. Four members (N, M) = (20,10), (55,20), (100,50), (200,100). Member state is a two-level latch computed on the daily frame: ON when daily close > max(daily high of the N days before the current day) ; OFF when daily close < min(daily low of the M days before the current day) ; otherwise the previous state is kept ; undefined (NaN) channel values give OFF. Channels use rolling(N).max().shift(1) and rolling(M).min().shift(1) on the daily frame before the merge. ENTRY: at least 2 of the 4 members are ON and the strategy is flat (level entry, including the first candle of a split if already ON). EXIT: fewer than 2 members ON. No stop-loss (stoploss attribute -0.99 only to satisfy the engine contract), no take-profit, no sizing hooks, no cooldown. Exposure is the fixed share chosen by the lab rule (bootstrap.choose_exposure on exploration trades). Sensitivities reported, never selectable: threshold 1 and 3 of 4, each member alone, the vote without member (20,10), thresholds counted in the DSR N. Warm-up: 400 bars (daily candles; the state at the first day of a split was verified equal to the full-history state for 400, 600 and 900 days on BTC, ETH and BNB). | Protocol v3 and exploration-only audit facts; no validation or holdout result was used for this rule. | 0 | dropped: fewer than 30 trades [code archived] | Donchian ensemble: majority vote of four daily channels, no stop. | H-027 | 2026-11-05T20:27:07.052658+00:00 | DailySupertrendFlip | Signals use CLOSED DAILY candles (UTC) built from the 1h data (informative 1d merged with merge_informative_pair, forward-filled, so a daily value is visible only after that day has closed); orders execute on the 1h chart at the next 1h open. Long only, one position per pair, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, no take-profit, no sizing hooks, no cooldown, no other filters. Daily definitions (d = just-closed day, included): TR(d) = max(high-low, |high-close(d-1)|, |low-close(d-1)|); ATR(d) = Wilder smoothing of TR with length 10 (alpha 1/10, seeded with the mean of the first 10 TRs); hl2 = (high+low)/2; BasicUpper = hl2 + 3.0*ATR; BasicLower = hl2 - 3.0*ATR. FinalUpper(d) = BasicUpper(d) if BasicUpper(d) < FinalUpper(d-1) or close(d-1) > FinalUpper(d-1), else FinalUpper(d-1). FinalLower(d) = BasicLower(d) if BasicLower(d) > FinalLower(d-1) or close(d-1) < FinalLower(d-1), else FinalLower(d-1). Trend state: initial state is DOWN on the first day with a defined ATR. If state(d-1) is DOWN and close(d) > FinalUpper(d) the state becomes UP; if state(d-1) is UP and close(d) < FinalLower(d) the state becomes DOWN; otherwise it is unchanged. The Supertrend line is FinalLower in UP and FinalUpper in DOWN. ENTRY when a daily candle closes and the state flips from DOWN to UP on that day, and no position is open (flip only; a state that is already UP is not an entry, including on the first candle of a split). EXIT when a daily candle closes and the state flips from UP to DOWN (daily close below the Supertrend line). EXIT: sell at the open of the 21:00 UTC bar (signal at the close of the 20:00 UTC bar), i.e. a holding period of 8 hourly bars (13:00-21:00). Also force the exit if the position is still open at that time for any reason; never hold overnight. STOP-LOSS: fixed 3% below the entry price, native intrabar stop (triggers as soon as price touches it). No take-profit, no pyramiding, no other filters, no cooldown (the entry is at most once per day by construction). Weekday definition: day-of-week of the candle open time in UTC, Saturday=5, Sunday=6. Expected trades: about 104 weekend days per year, UPTREND true on roughly half to two thirds, so about 50-70 trades per year per pair. Let lo = min low, hi = max high of those bars, width = (hi-lo)/50; 50 equal rows, row k covers [lo+k*width, lo+(k+1)*width), top row includes hi. Each bar's volume is spread equally over every row its [low,high] range overlaps (a bar with high==low goes to the row containing it). POC = row with the largest volume (ties: lowest row); POC price = centre of that row. Value area: start with the POC row, repeatedly compare the summed volume of the next two rows above vs the next two rows below the area (if only one row remains on a side, use it; ties: choose below) and add the heavier pair, until the area holds >= 70% of total volume. VAL = bottom edge of the lowest row in the value area. VAL and POC are frozen for all bars of day D (signal bars 00:00..23:00). ENTRY at close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) min(low over bars t-3..t) < VAL; (2) close(t) > VAL; (3) close(t-1) <= VAL OR low(t) < VAL (first reclaim); (4) POC/close(t) - 1 >= 0.015; (5) no open position; (6) no entry already signalled on the same UTC day; (7) cooldown satisfied. At entry record sweep_low = min(low over t-3..t), fixed. EXIT at close of bar t (executed next open) when any: close(t) >= POC (the frozen POC of the current day); 48 bars elapsed since entry; close(t) < sweep_low*0.995. Buy at the next bar open. EXIT, whichever comes first: (a) TIME EXIT: 24 hours after the entry fill, i.e. the exit signal is raised at the close of the 24th bar of the trade (the bar opening 23 hours after the entry bar) and the position is sold at the next open, so no position is held longer than 24 hours; (b) FAILURE EXIT: a bar closes below O, the open of the UTC day on which the entry signal fired (the move has fully failed); sell at the next open. STOP-LOSS: fixed 4% below the entry price, native intrabar stop (a disaster stop only; exit (b) normally triggers first). No cooldown beyond the one-entry-per-UTC-day rule, no take-profit, no trailing, no pyramiding, no other filters. ASIAN RANGE for UTC day D: AH = highest high and AL = lowest low of the 28 bars of day D opening 00:00, 00:15, ..., 06:45 UTC. If any of these 28 bars is missing, day D is not armed. DAILY FILTER: ATR14 = ATR(14) with Wilder smoothing (RMA, alpha 1/14) of true range, computed on closed UTC daily candles (informative 1d via merge_informative_pair, only completed days, so the value used on day D is the one at the close of day D-1). Day D is ARMED only if 0.25 <= (AH - AL) / ATR14 <= 0.80. ENTRY WINDOW: the 12 bars of day D opening 07:00 through 09:45 UTC. Scan these bars in time order on their closes: if a bar closes below AL before any bar has closed above AH, there is no trade on day D (first break is downward). Otherwise, at the first bar in the window whose close > AH, buy at the next bar open (latest possible fill: the 10:00 open). At most one trade per UTC day; no re-entry that day after any exit. STOP: a price stop at AL, intrabar (fills as soon as the low touches AL; if a bar opens below AL, fill at the open), implemented with custom_stoploss as a fixed price level for the trade. Native fixed backstop stop-loss: 8% below entry price (intrabar), only a safety net since AL is always closer given the width filter on these pairs. TARGET: T = entry + (entry - AL), entry = actual fill price (1R); exit as a limit when a bar high reaches T, filled at T (if a bar opens at or above T, fill at that open). TIME EXIT: if neither stop nor target is hit, sell at the open of the 12:00 UTC bar of day D (exit signal on the close of the 11:45 bar). Longest hold about 5 hours, always under 24 hours. No cooldown beyond one trade per day. No other filters. Warm-up: 4320 bars (45 daily candles x 96 bars, generous for the recursive Wilder ATR(14); the intraday range needs at most 40 bars). | No price data inspected; only the context file research/context/H-067.md (exploration summaries of earlier hypotheses on BTC, ETH, BNB) and the cited web pages, as of 2026-11-07. | 0 | failed: causality checks [code archived] | Asian-session (00-07 UTC) range breakout at the European open on 15m, 1R target, flat by 12:00 UTC |

DEFINITIONS (completed bars only): EXPECTED VOLUME Vexp(t) = arithmetic mean of volume at bars t-24, t-48, ..., t-480 (the 20 bars at the same UTC hour on the 20 previous days; the current day and bar t are excluded). In pandas: mean of volume.shift(24*k) for k = 1..20; if any of the 20 values is missing (NaN) or Vexp(t) = 0, no signal. VOLUME SURPRISE VS(t) = volume(t) / Vexp(t). BAR RETURN B(t) = close(t)/open(t) - 1. CLOSE LOCATION CL(t) = (close(t) - low(t)) / (high(t) - low(t)); if high(t) == low(t) there is no signal. ENTRY at the close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) VS(t) >= 2.0; (2) B(t) >= 0.006; (3) CL(t) >= 0.7; (4) no position is open. Buy at the open of bar t+1. At signal time store LOWSIG = low(t), fixed for the trade. EXITS, the first of: (a) TIME EXIT: the exit signal is raised at the close of bar t+12 (the 12th bar held, counting the entry-fill bar t+1 as the first) and the position is sold at the open of bar t+13, i.e. after 12 full bars; no position lasts more than 12 hours, well under 24 hours. (b) SIGNAL-BAR-LOW EXIT: if any bar u >= t+1 (the entry-fill bar included) closes below LOWSIG, sell at the open of bar u+1. (c) STOP-LOSS: one fixed native intrabar stop 2.0% below the entry price (stoploss = -0.02), triggered as soon as price touches it. Note on the stop: the idea's stop is the low of bar t checked intrabar, which varies per trade; the engine requires one fixed percentage, so the intrabar stop is fixed at 2.0% (an up bar of at least 0.6% that closes in its top 30% usually has its low roughly 0.7-2% below the close) and the idea's level is kept as the closed-bar exit (b). No take-profit, no trailing stop, no trend, daily or other filter, no cooldown other than one position at a time (a new entry can be signalled only on a bar close after the previous position has been closed). Single cell, no grid explored. If any of the 97 required bars is missing (fewer than 97 consecutive 15m bars spanning 23:45 of d-1 to 23:45 of d) or RV = 0, there is no signal for day d+1. ENTRY: at the close of the 23:45 UTC bar of day d, if RSK <= -0.25 and no position is open, emit a buy signal; it fills at the open of the 00:00 UTC bar of day d+1. EXIT (time exit): at the close of the 23:30 UTC bar of day d+1, emit a sell signal; it fills at the open of the 23:45 UTC bar of day d+1 (95 bars, 23h45m after entry; equivalently, exit once the trade has been open 95 bars). The time exit is the only exit. Stop-loss: none by design (implement as a native stoploss of -0.99, i.e. 99% below entry, which is never meant to bind). No take-profit, no trailing stop, no trend filter, no cooldown (entries happen only at 00:00 UTC, so positions cannot overlap or chain; the exit at 23:45 leaves the 00:00 bar free for the next signal). Same rule and parameters on all nine pairs. Long only, one position, whole balance, same rule and parameters on every pair. Definitions: M(t) = close(t)/close(t-24) - 1 (24-bar momentum). S(t) = sample standard deviation (ddof=1) of the 720 values M(t-720), ..., M(t-1) (rolling 720-bar std of M, shifted by one bar so the current bar is excluded). ATR(t) = Wilder ATR with period 14 on 1h bars (true range smoothed with alpha = 1/14). ENTRY at the close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) M(t) >= 1.5 * S(t); (2) M(t) >= 0.02; (3) close(t) > close(t-1); (4) no open position; (5) cooldown satisfied: no entry for 6 candles after any exit (at least 6 bars since the last exit). TRAILING EXIT (close-based, an exit signal, not the native stop): let e be the entry bar (the bar whose open is the fill) and P the fill price. Initial trail level T = P - 2.5 * ATR(t), with t the signal bar. At the close of every bar j >= e: if close(j) < T, exit at the open of bar j+1; otherwise update T = max(T, close(j) - 2.5 * ATR(j)), so T only moves up. TIME EXIT: if still open, exit at the close of bar e+23 (the 24th bar of the trade, filled at the open of bar e+24), so every position is closed within 24 hours. Whichever of the trailing exit and the time exit comes first applies. STOP-LOSS: native fixed stop 8% below the entry price (intrabar, triggers on touch); it is a catastrophic backstop only, since the 2.5-ATR trail normally sits much closer. No take-profit, no ROI table, no pyramiding, no other filters. Long only, one position, whole balance. POLE CANDIDATES: for each p with t-9 <= p <= t-3 (flag of 2 to 8 bars), require high(p) = highest high of bars p..t-1. PL(p) = lowest low of bars p-5..p (6 bars); H(p) = high(p) - PL(p); require high(p)/PL(p) - 1 >= 0.025. FLAG = bars p+1..t-1. FL = lowest low of the flag, FH = highest high of the flag. Conditions: (1) depth: FL >= high(p) - 0.5*H; (2) tightness: FH - FL <= 0.5*H; (3) volume: mean volume of the flag bars <= 0.7 * mean volume of bars p-5..p; (4) trend: close(t) > SMA(168) of 1h closes at t. If several p satisfy all of (pole, 1-3), use the most recent p (largest p). ENTRY at the close of t when close(t) > high(p) for that p AND condition (4) AND no open position AND that pole (identified by the timestamp of bar p) has not already been traded (store it in the entry tag; reject a new entry with the same pole timestamp). Store FL and TARGET = FL + H in the entry tag, fixed at entry. A signal at a bar close is filled at the next bar open. Spot, long only, one position per pair, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, no sizing hooks, no other filters (no 1h/4h/1d filter). DAYS: trade only when the UTC date is Monday to Friday. OPENING RANGE of a UTC day: the two 15m bars opening at 13:30 and 13:45 UTC. RH = max of their highs, RL = min of their lows, M = (RH + RL) / 2. If either bar is missing or has zero volume, the day is not armed. RH, RL and M are frozen for the day. ARMED: the day is armed only if (RH - RL) / RL >= 0.02. ENTRY: on an armed day, the FIRST 15m bar whose open time is between 14:00 and 17:45 UTC inclusive and whose close > RH gives the entry signal; buy at the next bar open (latest fill 18:00 UTC). Later bars of the same day never signal, so there is at most one entry per day per pair and no new entry that day after any exit. STOP (per-trade, intrabar): at entry, the stop price is fixed at M and is never moved. This is a constant fraction s = (fill - M) / fill below the fill price for that trade (s >= about 1% because RH - M >= 1% of RL). It triggers intrabar as soon as the price touches M or lower, filled at M (or at the bar open if the bar opens below M). If fill <= M, exit at once. Safety net: the engine's native fixed stoploss is -10% below entry, used only if M cannot be read. TAKE PROFIT (intrabar): T = fill + 2.0 x (fill - M). Exit as soon as the price touches T, filled at T. TIME EXIT: the close of the 15m bar opening at 19:45 UTC on the entry day gives an exit signal, sold at the 20:00 UTC open whatever happens. As a safety against missing bars, any bar with open time >= 19:45 UTC on the entry date, or on a later date, also triggers the exit. Holding is at most 5h45m (entry 14:15 at the earliest, exit 20:00), under the 12-hour limit. If stop and target are touched in the same bar, the stop counts first. Cooldown: none beyond the one-entry-per-day rule. No indicators. Longest lookback is the 13:30 bar, 17 bars before a 17:45 signal bar. Warm-up: 96 bars. | No candle data was inspected. | H-093 | 2026-11-10T03:34:03.428554+00:00 | TDSetupExhaustion | Chart 15m, closed bars only. A signal at a bar close fills at the next bar's open. Each pair is traded on its own. No indicators. Warm-up: 100 bars (the longest lookback is about 21 bars: 5 before the flip plus up to 15 after it; 100 bars is a safe margin).
1) BEARISH PRICE FLIP at bar f: close(f) < close(f-4) AND close(f-1) > close(f-5). Bar f is setup count 1.
2) BUY SETUP: bars f+1..f+8 (counts 2..9) each have close < close 4 bars earlier. If a bar fails this before count 9, the setup is cancelled; a new one starts only at a later flip. Name nK = f+K-1, so n6=f+5, n7=f+6, n8=f+7, n9=f+8. A setup ends at count 9 (no count beyond 9). Setups are tracked one at a time; the timings make overlap impossible.
3) DECLINE FILTER, checked at n9: close(f-1)/close(n9) - 1 >= 0.015, else no trade.
4) PERFECTION: P = min(low(n6), low(n7)). Perfected at n9 if min(low(n8), low(n9)) <= P; the perfecting bar p = n9. If not, the first bar among n9+1..n9+4 with low <= P is p. If none, no trade.
5) ENTRY TRIGGER: the first bar t in p..p+3 (p included, 4 bars) with close(t) > high(t-1). None: no trade. The signal is valid only if no trade is open on the pair. Buy at open(t+1) = entry.
6) TRADE CHECKS at the fill (confirm_trade_entry, with entry = the fill rate): LL = lowest low of bars f..t. STOP = min(LL*0.998, entry*0.99), so it is always at least 1% below entry. Skip if STOP < entry*0.97 (more than 3% below). TP = highest high of setup bars f..n9. Skip if TP < entry*1.01.
7) EXITS: (a) Stop: STOP is a fixed price level set at entry and never moved. It triggers intrabar as soon as low <= STOP (custom_stoploss via stoploss_from_absolute; the native stoploss = -0.03 is the backstop). (b) Take-profit: exit at TP intrabar as soon as high >= TP. DEAD: any window bar t after the first sweep bar with close(t) < 0.995*SL(t-1) kills the setup (no trade). RECLAIM: the first window bar t, at or after the first sweep bar, setup not dead, with t-K(t) <= 4 bars AND close(t) > RL AND high(t) > low(t) AND (close(t)-low(t))/(high(t)-low(t)) >= 0.5. Signal at close of t; buy at the open of t+1 (fill P) only if no position is open; otherwise the setup is consumed with no trade. The first reclaim consumes the setup (one trade per setup, at most 2 per day). FILL CHECKS (confirm_trade_entry, P = fill rate, SL = SL(t)): skip if 0.998*SL < 0.97*P (stop more than 3% away), or if RH < 1.01*P. Native stoploss -0.03 as backstop only. TAKE-PROFIT: limit at RH, filled at RH when a bar high reaches it (at the open if a bar opens above RH). If stop and RH are touched in the same bar, the stop counts first. Max hold: A fills 07:15-11:00 and exits by 15:00; L fills 13:45-17:00 and exits by 21:00, so at most 7h45m. No trailing, no cooldown beyond one trade per setup, single cell, no grid. INDICATORS (all windows include the current closed bar t): Tenkan T(t) = (max high of bars t-19..t + min low of bars t-19..t)/2 (20 bars). Kijun K(t) = same over bars t-59..t (60 bars). SpanA_raw(t) = (T(t)+K(t))/2. SpanB_raw(t) = (max high + min low of bars t-119..t)/2 (120 bars). Displacement 30: the cloud at bar t is SpanA(t) = SpanA_raw(t-30) and SpanB(t) = SpanB_raw(t-30); CloudTop(t) = max(SpanA(t), SpanB(t)). Lagging-line confirmation: close(t) > close(t-30). ENTRY at close of bar t when flat and all of: close(t) > CloudTop(t) AND close(t) > close(t-30) AND at least one of (A) TK cross: T(t-1) <= K(t-1) and T(t) > K(t); or (B) cloud break: close(t-1) <= CloudTop(t-1) and T(t) > K(t). Buy at open of bar t+1. EXITS, whichever comes first: (1) native hard stop-loss 3% below the entry fill price, triggered intrabar as soon as price touches it (stoploss = -0.03); (2) Kijun exit: a closed 1h bar with close < K of that bar, sold at the next bar open; (3) time exit: when the trade has been open 24 hours (i.e. at the close of the 24th 1h bar counted from the entry bar, the entry bar being bar 1), sold at the next open, so no position is held more than 24 hours. No take-profit, no trailing stop, no cooldown, no pyramiding; a new entry may be taken on any later bar that meets the entry condition. Warm-up: 150 bars (120-bar SpanB plus 30-bar displacement; all indicators are rolling max/min, non-recursive). ADDED RULE (the only change from H-084): the ENTRY also requires relative volume RV(t) >= 2.0 at the signal bar t, where RV(t) = volume(t) / mean(volume(t-120..t-1)) (the previous 120 closed 1h bars, the current bar excluded); no other change: exits, stops, timing, windows and the 24-hour/day limits are exactly those of H-084. The 2.0 and the 120 are fixed ex ante (twice the 5-day mean volume), no grid. | Exploration-split trade statistics of H-084 only (research/learnings digest and its backtest artifacts). Long only, spot, one position per pair, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, no take-profit, no sizing hooks, no other filters. DEFINITIONS: r(i) = ln(close(i)/close(i-1)). Local volatility s(t) = sqrt((pi/2) * mean over i = t-96..t-1 of |r(i)|*|r(i-1)|) (bipower variation over the previous 96 bars; the current bar t is excluded; needs closes t-98..t-1). Jump statistic J(t) = r(t)/s(t); if s(t) is 0 or undefined there is no signal. DAILY FILTER: build UTC daily candles from the 15m data (informative 1d via merge_informative_pair, forward-filled, so a daily close is visible only after its day has closed). The filter holds when the last completed daily close (the previous UTC day) is strictly above the simple mean of the last 30 completed daily closes (that day included). ENTRY at the close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) J(t) >= 4.0; (2) r(t) >= 0.006; (3) bar t opens between 00:00 and 17:45 UTC inclusive; (4) daily filter true; (5) no position open; (6) no trade has already been entered on this UTC day (the day of bar t). Store L = low of the jump bar t at signal time. EXIT, first of: (a) on any 15m bar u >= t+1 (the entry bar included) whose close < L, sell at the open of bar u+1; (b) time exit: at the close of the 23:45 UTC bar of the signal day, sell at the open of the 00:00 UTC bar of the next day, so every position is closed within 24 hours. STOP-LOSS: one fixed 3% below the entry price, the engine's native intrabar stop (a catastrophic backstop; exit (a) is the designed loss exit). COOLDOWN: none beyond the one-trade-per-UTC-day rule. All numbers fixed ex ante, no grid: return window 1 bar, bipower window 96 bars, jump threshold 4.0, minimum bar return 0.006 log, entry window 00:00-17:45 UTC, daily mean 30 days, time exit 23:45 bar close, stop 3%. Warm-up: 3072 bars (30 completed daily closes need up to 31 UTC days = 2976 15m bars; the bipower window needs 98 bars; 3072 = 32 days gives margin; no recursive indicators; under the 4500 limit). Long only, one position, whole allowed balance. DAILY STATISTICS (built from completed 1h bars, no lookahead): for each completed UTC day k, C(k) = close of that day's 23:00 bar (if that bar is missing, the close of the last bar of that day). Daily return R(k) = C(k)/C(k-1) - 1. For the current UTC day d, m = mean and s = sample standard deviation (ddof=1) of R(k) over the 30 completed days k = d-30 .. d-1 (needs 31 daily closes = 744 bars); m and s are fixed for the whole of day d. INTRADAY: O = open of day d's 00:00 bar (if missing, the open of the first bar of day d). D(t) = close(t)/O - 1 for every bar t of day d. Threshold T = m + 0.75*s. ENTRY at the close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) the open time of bar t is between 01:00 and 18:00 UTC inclusive; (2) D(t) >= T; (3) D(t) >= 0.01; (4) D(t-1) < T, where t-1 is the previous bar of the same UTC day (first crossing); (5) no position is open; (6) no entry has been made earlier in the same UTC day (at most one entry per UTC day). Buy at the next bar open. EXIT, whichever comes first: (a) TIME EXIT: 24 hours after the entry fill, i.e. the exit signal is raised at the close of the 24th bar of the trade (the bar opening 23 hours after the entry bar) and the position is sold at the next open, so no position is held longer than 24 hours; (b) FAILURE EXIT: a bar closes below O, the open of the UTC day on which the entry signal fired (the move has fully failed); sell at the next open. STOP-LOSS: fixed 4% below the entry price, native intrabar stop (a disaster stop only; exit (b) normally triggers first). No cooldown beyond the one-entry-per-UTC-day rule, no take-profit, no trailing, no pyramiding, no other filters. Warm-up: 800 bars (744 bars for 31 daily closes plus up to 24 bars of the current day, rounded up; no recursive indicators are used). | H-105 | 2026-11-11T00:22:20.456137+00:00 | DailyMaxJumpMomentum | Chart 1h, closed bars only, all times UTC; a signal at a bar close is filled at the next 1h open. Spot, long only, one position per pair, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, no take-profit, no other filters. BLOCKS: two-hour blocks aligned to even UTC hours; block b = the 1h bar opening at an even hour H plus the bar opening at H+1. Block close C(b) = close of the H+1 bar; a block exists only when both bars exist. Block values are computed only at the close of the odd-hour bar (block complete) and held (forward-filled) on the next bar; nothing uses an unfinished block. DEFINITIONS on block closes, b = the just-completed block: r(b) = C(b)/C(b-1) - 1 (close-to-close block return). J(b) = max of r over the 30 blocks b-29..b (b included). Jump regime ON when J(b) >= median of the 150 values J(b-150)..J(b-1) (b excluded). SMA50(b) = mean of C(b-49..b); SMA20(b) = mean of C(b-19..b); HH10(b) = max of C(b-10..b-1) (b excluded). ENTRY, evaluated only at a block close (close of an odd-hour 1h bar), all must hold: (1) jump regime ON; (2) C(b) > SMA50(b); (3) C(b) > HH10(b); (4) no open position; (5) cooldown satisfied; (6) the buy fills at the open of the next block, and that block's open time must be between 00:00 and 16:00 UTC inclusive (i.e. signal blocks opening 22:00 of the previous day, 00:00, 02:00, ..., 14:00). EXIT, first of: (a) at a block close with C(b) < SMA20(b), sell at the next 1h open; (b) time exit, session: the close of the 22:00 bar raises the exit, sell at the 23:00 UTC open; (c) time exit, 12 hours: the close of the bar opening 11 hours after the entry fill raises the exit, sell at the open 12 hours after the fill; (d) STOP-LOSS: one fixed 2% below the entry price, the engine's native intrabar stop. TWO-HOUR BLOCKS: built inside the strategy from the 1h bars; a block is the pair of 1h bars opening at an even UTC hour H (00,02,...,22) and at H+1. Block open = open of the H bar, high = max of the two highs, low = min of the two lows, close = close of the H+1 bar. A block exists only if both 1h bars are present; a missing bar means that block is skipped. A block is complete at the close of its H+1 bar (time H+2); its values are written on the H+1 row and forward-filled to later 1h rows, so nothing is visible before the block has closed. Block indicators (b = the just-closed block, included), computed on the series of complete blocks: EMA20 = EMA of block close, span 20 (alpha 2/21), seeded with the first block close; TR(b) = max(high-low, |high-close(b-1)|, |low-close(b-1)|); ATR20 = Wilder smoothing of TR (alpha 1/20), seeded with the mean of the first 20 TRs; UpperBand(b) = EMA20(b) + 1.5*ATR20(b); SMA100(b) = mean of the last 100 block closes including b. ENTRY (evaluated only at the close of the H+1 bar, i.e. when a block closes): close(b) > UpperBand(b) AND close(b-1) <= UpperBand(b-1) (fresh cross) AND close(b) > SMA100(b) AND block b opened at 00:00, 02:00, ..., or 16:00 UTC (H between 0 and 16 inclusive) AND no open position. Order fills at the next 1h open (H+2, so fills fall between 02:00 and 18:00 UTC). EXITS, first to occur: (1) a block closes with close(b) < EMA20(b): exit at the next 1h open; (2) STOP-LOSS: native intrabar stop fixed at 2% below the entry fill price (stoploss = -0.02), never moved; (3) SESSION EXIT: exit signal at the close of the 1h bar opening 22:00 UTC, filled at the 23:00 UTC open; (4) TIME EXIT: exit at the first 1h open at least 12 hours after the entry fill (e.g. minimal_roi {"720": -1} or an equivalent custom_exit). No position is ever held past 23:00 UTC of its entry day. | H-110 | 2026-11-11T02:42:00.381950+00:00 | InverseFVGReclaim | Chart 15m, UTC, closed bars only; a signal at the close of bar t fills at the open of bar t+1. No higher-timeframe filter. Warm-up: 30 bars (longest lookback is the swing-low window k-2..t, at most 27 bars; no recursive indicators).
GAP DETECTION at the close of bar k: a bearish FVG exists if high(k) < low(k-2). Gap top GT = low(k-2), gap bottom GB = high(k). The gap is valid only if (GT-GB)/GB >= 0.004 AND the middle bar k-1 is a down displacement: close(k-1) < open(k-1) AND open(k-1)/close(k-1) - 1 >= 0.01. Only the most recent valid bearish gap is tracked: a new valid gap replaces any older tracked gap, which is dropped.
INVERSION SIGNAL: for bars t = k+1 .. k+24 (inclusive, 6 hours), the signal is the close of the FIRST bar t with close(t) > GT AND close(t) > open(t). A wick above GT without such a close does not count. If no such bar occurs by the close of bar k+24, the gap is dropped. The gap is consumed at its first qualifying bar t whether or not a trade is opened (one entry per gap).
RISK FILTER: swing low SL = lowest low of bars k-2 .. t inclusive. R = close(t) - SL. Trade only if 0.008 <= R/close(t) <= 0.03; otherwise skip and drop the gap.
ENTRY: buy at open(t+1) only if flat on that pair, fewer than 3 entries already opened on that pair in the current UTC day (counted by entry fill time), and open(t+1) > SL (else skip).
STOP: fixed price SL, frozen at entry; equivalently a fixed stop of (entry_price - SL)/entry_price below the entry price, set once per trade (custom_stoploss returning that frozen distance), touched intrabar. Native stoploss backstop -0.035.
TAKE PROFIT: limit sell at TP = close(t) + 1.5*R, touched intrabar (fill at TP, or at the bar open if the bar opens above TP).
If stop and TP are both touched in the same bar, assume the stop.
TIME EXIT: if still open, sell at the open of the bar 48 bars (12 hours) after the entry bar. Spot, long only, one position per pair, no pyramiding. HOURLY BARS: a 1h bar for clock hour h on UTC day d is built from the four completed 15m bars that open at h:00, h:15, h:30 and h:45 (high = max high, low = min low, close = close of the h:45 bar). It is complete at the close of the h:45 bar. range(H) = high(H) - low(H). NR4 SETUP: a completed 1h bar H (hour h, day d) is a session-aware NR4 when range(H) is strictly smaller than the range of the 1h bar at the same clock hour h on each of days d-1, d-2 and d-3 (all three must exist and be complete), AND range(H)/close(H) >= 0.0025. FILTER (higher-timeframe direction): 4h blocks are built from 16 completed 15m bars, starting at 00, 04, 08, 12, 16 and 20 UTC. At the signal bar's close, the close of the last fully completed 4h block must be above the SMA(50) of the last 50 completed 4h closes, including that block. ENTRY WINDOW: the 8 15m bars that follow H, opening at (h+1):00 through (h+2):45. If several NR4 windows overlap, only the most recently completed NR4 bar H is used. ENTRY: the first 15m bar in the window that meets all of these: close > high(H); the filter holds; the pair is flat; fewer than 2 entries already made on this pair on the signal bar's UTC date; and low(H) >= 0.98 x close(signal bar), i.e. skip the trade if low(H) is more than 2% below. Buy at the next open. One entry at most per NR4 bar. STOP-LOSS (intrabar): a percentage fixed once at entry, stop_pct = max(0.01, (entry - low(H))/entry), capped at 0.02. Stop price = entry x (1 - stop_pct), constant for the life of the trade (equal to min(low(H), 0.99 x entry)). Implement it as a custom stop relative to the open rate, with low(H) carried in the enter_tag. The native hard stoploss is -0.02. TARGET: target = entry x (1 + 2 x stop_pct), i.e. 2R. Exit at the next open after the first 15m close >= target. TIME EXIT: at the close of the 32nd 15m bar after entry, counting the entry bar as bar 1, exit at the next open (8 hours). No other exits, filters or cooldown. Warm-up: 900 bars (SMA(50) of 4h closes = 800 15m bars, plus 16 bars of 4h-block alignment, plus margin; the 3-day same-hour lookback is 292 bars). | No candle data inspected. Spot, long only, one position per pair, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, same numbers on all nine pairs, no higher-timeframe filter. GAP: for three consecutive bars k-1, k, k+1, a bullish fair value gap exists when low(k+1) > high(k-1). Bottom GL = high(k-1), top GH = low(k+1), midpoint CE = (GL+GH)/2. The gap is VALID only if all hold: (1) (GH-GL)/close(k+1) >= 0.003; (2) close(k) > open(k); (3) volume(k) >= 2.0 x arithmetic mean of volume over the 20 bars k-20..k-1 (bar k excluded; activity filter b on the displacement bar). A valid gap becomes known at the close of bar k+1. TRACKING: at the close of bar t the tracked gap is the most recent valid gap with k+1 <= t-1 (a newer valid gap replaces an older one; no fallback to an older gap once the newest is dead). It is ACTIVE on bars t = k+2 .. k+33 (32 bars) unless it was already consumed or invalidated. INVALIDATION: the first bar whose close is below GL kills the gap (from that bar on). CONSUMPTION: the gap is consumed at the first bar t with a retest signal, whether or not an order fills (one signal per gap). ENTRY signal at the close of bar t when the tracked gap is active and low(t) <= CE and close(t) > GL. The order fills at open(t+1) only if the pair is flat and fewer than 4 trades have been opened on this pair during the UTC calendar day of the fill (counted by fill time; confirm_trade_entry). STOP-LOSS: d = max(1 - 0.998 x GL / E, 0.01), computed at the fill from GL (carried with the signal) and the fill price E, frozen for the trade; stop price E x (1 - d) = min(0.998 x GL, 0.99 x E). It is a fixed distance below the entry price, triggered intrabar as soon as the low touches it (custom_stoploss returning the frozen d; native stoploss -0.10 is only an engine backstop). TAKE-PROFIT: target E x (1 + 2d) = E + 2 x (E - stop) (2R), sold at that price when a bar's high reaches it (at the open if a bar opens above it). If stop and target are touched in the same bar, the stop counts. TIME EXIT: with the fill at the open of bar e = t+1, exit signal at the close of bar e+47 (the 48th 15m bar of the trade), sold at the open of e+48, i.e. 12 hours after the fill. No trailing, no break-even move, no cooldown (a new signal may fill at the open right after an exit), no other filters, single grid cell. Zone OL = low(j), OH = high(j), mean threshold MT = (OH+OL)/2; store D. SETUP: known at the close of D; a displacement with an order block replaces any older setup, and bar D itself gives no entry signal. The setup is ACTIVE on bars t = D+1..D+32 unless invalidated or consumed. INVALIDATION: the first bar t > D with close(t) < OL kills it. CONSUMPTION: the first bar meeting entry conditions (a)-(c) consumes it, whether or not an order fills (one signal per setup). ENTRY signal at the close of bar t when the setup is active and (a) low(t) <= MT; (b) close(t) > OL; (c) ATR(t)/close(t) >= 0.004 (activity filter a). No trailing, no break-even move, no cooldown, no other filters. | H-119 | 2026-11-11T07:05:37.830013+00:00 | EfficiencyRatioThrust | Chart 15m, closed bars only, same numbers on all nine pairs, one position per pair, no higher-timeframe filter. A signal is computed at the close of bar t and filled at the open of bar t+1 (entry price E). INDICATORS: signed efficiency ratio ER(t) = (close(t) - close(t-10)) / sum for i=0..9 of |close(t-i) - close(t-i-1)| (10 bars; ER(t) = 0 when the denominator is 0). ATR(t) = Wilder ATR(14) of 15m bars (true range, Wilder smoothing alpha 1/14). ENTRY at the close of bar t when ALL hold: (1) ER(t-1) <= 0.6 AND ER(t) > 0.6 (fresh cross into the efficient-uptrend zone); (2) ATR(t)/close(t) >= 0.004 (activity filter a, fixed in advance); (3) no open position on the pair. Because condition (1) is a cross, after any exit a new entry always needs a new cross from at or below 0.6; a cross that occurs while a position is open is ignored. STOP (price level, executed intrabar as soon as the low touches it, implemented via custom_stoploss returning the distance from the current rate to the stored level): initial stop S0 = min(lowest low of bars t-9..t, 0.99*E), so it is always at least 1% below entry. After the close of each later bar k in the trade, S = max(S, lowest low of bars k-9..k): the stop is raised to the 10-bar low when that is higher and is never lowered; the new level applies from bar k+1 (if bar k+1 opens below it, the exit fills at that open). Native fixed backstop stoploss = -10% below E (a safety bound that should essentially never bind; the rule's stop is the trailing level). TAKE-PROFIT: sell at E*1.02 intrabar when the high reaches it (minimal_roi {"0": 0.02}). TIME EXIT: sell at the open of the first bar that starts 32 bars (8 hours) after the entry fill, i.e. after 32 bars in the trade, if neither stop nor target fired first. No cooldown beyond the fresh-cross requirement. If stop and target are both touched inside the same bar, the engine's default (stop first) applies. Same rule and numbers on all nine pairs; no higher-timeframe filter. Warm-up: 2112 bars (22 UTC days; longest lookback = 20 complete previous UTC days plus the current day = 2016 bars, rounded up for safety; no recursive indicators). DEFINITIONS: Day d = the 96 bars opening 00:00-23:45 UTC. Od = open of day d's 00:00 bar. k(t) = clock slot of bar t (0 for 00:00 ... 95 for 23:45). CUMVOL(d,k) = sum of volume of day d's bars from slot 0 through slot k. RVOL(t) = CUMVOL(d,k(t)) / mean over the 20 previous UTC days j=d-1..d-20 of CUMVOL(j,k(t)) (time-of-day matched, day d excluded). If any of those 20 days lacks a bar at or before slot k, or the mean is 0, RVOL is undefined and no entry is allowed. HOD(t) = highest high of day d's bars from slot 0 through bar t-1; s(t) = index of the most recent bar in that window whose high equals HOD(t); the level is 'held' only if s(t) <= t-8 (set at least 8 bars, 2 h, before t). ENTRY (all must hold at the close of bar t): (1) bar t opens between 02:00 and 19:45 UTC inclusive; (2) close(t) > HOD(t) and the held condition s(t) <= t-8; (3) RVOL(t) >= 1.5 (activity filter b, volume); (4) close(t)/Od - 1 >= 0.005; (5) no open position on the pair; (6) fewer than 2 entries already filled on that pair with entry date in day d. CLOCK: convert each bar's UTC open time to America/New_York local time (DST per date, e.g. zoneinfo). WINDOWS: three per NY calendar day, the four bars whose NY open time is 03:00, 03:15, 03:30, 03:45 (W1), 10:00-10:45 (W2) and 14:00-14:45 (W3). For a window let w0 be its first bar and w3 its last. POOL: PL = lowest low of the 16 bars w0-16..w0-1 (the 4 h ending just before the window opens). If any of those 16 bars or any window bar is missing, the window gives no trade. SWEEP: j = the first bar in w0-8..w3 with low(j) < PL (literal scout rule; since bars w0-8..w0-1 lie inside the pool their lows cannot be below PL, so in practice j is a window bar). GAP: three consecutive bars k-1, k, k+1 with k and k+1 inside the window and k-1 >= j (the gap forms after the sweep; the sweep bar may be bar k-1), all of: low(k+1) > high(k-1); (low(k+1) - high(k-1)) / close(k+1) >= 0.0025; close(k) > open(k); volume(k) >= 1.5 x arithmetic mean of volume of bars k-20..k-1 (activity filter b). Only the FIRST such gap of the window (smallest k+1) is used; later gaps in the same window are ignored. GT = low(k+1), GB = high(k-1). S = lowest low of bars j..k+1, frozen at the close of k+1. RETEST: for bars t = k+2..k+9 (8 bars, may run past the window end), in order: if low(t) < S or close(t) < GB the setup is cancelled (checked first, also on a bar that would otherwise qualify); else if low(t) <= GT (and close(t) >= GB) bar t is the entry signal and the setup is consumed, whether or not an order fills. No signal by the close of k+9: the setup expires. At most one trade per window. FILL: buy at open(t+1) only if the pair is flat. STOP: price min(S, 0.99 x E); distance d = 1 - stop/E, computed at the fill and frozen (so d >= 1%), triggered intrabar as soon as the low touches it (custom_stoploss returning d; native stoploss -0.10 as engine backstop only). TARGET: E x (1 + 2d) = E + 2 x (E - stop) (2R), sold at that price when a bar's high reaches it (at the open if a bar opens above it). If stop and target are touched in the same bar, the stop counts. TIME EXIT: with the fill at the open of bar e, exit signal at the close of bar e+31, sold at the open of e+32 (8 hours). TAKE-PROFIT: sell at E*1.015 (gross, before fees) when a bar high touches it. BLOCK-END LOSER EXIT: at the close of bar 16 of the entry block, if that close < E, sell at the next bar open (first bar of the next block). DEFINITIONS: MV(t) = mean volume of bars t-96..t-1; MR(t) = mean (high-low) of bars t-96..t-1 (24h, bar t excluded). CHURN bar c: volume(c) >= 2.0*MV(c) AND high(c)-low(c) <= 0.6*MR(c). CONTEXT (decline before the churn): close(c-32) - close(c) >= 3.0*MR(c). ZONE START: a churn bar c that meets CONTEXT and is not an extension of a live zone opens a zone with ZH = high(c), ZL = low(c), zone end z = c; it replaces any older unused zone. EXTENSION: a churn bar c' with 1 <= c' - z <= 4 (CONTEXT not required) extends the live zone: ZH = max(ZH, high(c')), ZL = min(ZL, low(c')), z = c'. VOID: the zone is cancelled if any bar after its start closes below ZL, or if no entry signal occurs by bar z+16. ENTRY: the first bar t with 1 <= t - z <= 16 such that close(t) > ZH AND high(t)-low(t) >= 1.5*MR(t) is the breakout bar. If ZL < 0.97*close(t) the zone is discarded with no trade; otherwise raise the entry signal at the close of t and buy at the open of t+1 (price E). If a position is already open on the pair at bar t, the zone is discarded. Each zone gives at most one breakout bar (used once). STOP: stop price S = min(ZL, 0.99*E), i.e. a per-trade fixed stop distance d = max(1%, (E-ZL)/E) below E, set at entry, never moved, triggered intrabar as soon as the low touches S (custom stoploss returning d; native safety stoploss -5%). TARGET: T = E + 1.5*(E - S), exit at T intrabar as soon as a bar's high reaches it; if stop and target are both touched in the same bar, assume the stop. TIME EXIT: if neither is hit, exit signal at the close of the 48th bar counted from the fill bar (fill bar = bar 1), filled at the open of the next (49th) bar, i.e. 12 hours after entry. No other exits, no trailing, no cooldown beyond one-position-per-pair and one-use zones. | No price data inspected; only the H-130 context file (exploration-only aggregates through 2026-11-13) and the cited web pages. | 0 | dropped: fewer than 30 trades [code archived] | 15m churn absorption after a decline: high-volume narrow-range zone, buy the wide-bar close above it DROPPED by the pre-registered rule: calibration trades {'BTC/USDT': 4, 'ETH/USDT': 6, 'BNB/USDT': 10, 'XRP/USDT': 2, 'ADA/USDT': 2, 'LTC/USDT': 5, 'LINK/USDT': 13, 'DOGE/USDT': 7, 'SOL/USDT': 2, 'AVAX/USDT': 4, 'DOT/USDT': 2, 'TRX/USDT': 2, 'NEAR/USDT': 2, 'UNI/USDT': 3, 'BCH/USDT': 7, 'XLM/USDT': 12, 'FIL/USDT': 3, 'AAVE/USDT': 5, 'INJ/USDT': 8, 'HBAR/USDT': 10, 'OP/USDT': 2, 'APT/USDT': 1, 'ARB/USDT': 1, 'SUI/USDT': 0, 'ATOM/USDT': 5, 'ETC/USDT': 10} (30 or more on 0 pair(s), 16 needed); not retuned. |

A newer setup bar replaces the active setup (only the latest P is tracked). H1: first bar i >= P+2 with high(i) > high(i-1) and high(i) < high(P); L1 = min(low of P+1..i-1). TARGET: T = E*(1 + 1.5*d), sold at T when a bar's high reaches it (at the open if a bar opens above it); stop counts if both touched in one bar. RE-ARM: after an exit filled at the open of bar x, only a setup bar P >= x can start a new setup. Spot, long only, one position per pair, no pyramiding, no sizing hooks, no other filters, every calendar day, same numbers on all pairs. SESSIONS: S1 = bars 00:00-07:45, S2 = 08:00-15:45, S3 = 16:00-23:45 (32 bars each, numbered 1-32). A session with any of its 32 bars missing is incomplete: it cannot be a spike session and does not count toward AR. SPIKE (evaluated at the close of bar 32 of a complete session X): BASE = max high of bars 1-24 of X; WH = max high and WL = min low of bars 25-32 (the last 2h); C = close of bar 32. AR = mean of (session high - session low, over all 32 bars) of the 10 most recent complete sessions of the same type (S1, S2 or S3) before X; if fewer than 10 exist, no spike. SPIKE is true if WH > BASE AND WH > WL AND C >= BASE + 0.25*AR AND (C - WL)/(WH - WL) >= 0.70. ACCEPTANCE (in the session N that immediately follows X; S3 is followed by the next day's S1): bars 1-4 of N must all exist; MID = (BASE + WH)/2; none of the closes of bars 1-4 is below MID, AND the close of bar 4 is above WH. Signal at the close of bar 4 of N; buy at the open of bar 5 of N (01:00, 09:00 or 17:00 UTC), fill price E, only if no position is open on the pair. FILL CHECK (confirm_trade_entry with the fill rate E): skip the trade if BASE < 0.97*E. STOP: SP = min(BASE, 0.99*E); d = (E - SP)/E (between 1% and 3%) is computed once at the fill and frozen: a fixed percentage below the entry price, intrabar (custom_stoploss returns d; it triggers as soon as price touches SP). Native stoploss -0.03 as backstop only. TAKE-PROFIT: TP = E + 1.5*(E - SP) (1.5%-4.5% above E), filled at TP when a bar high reaches it (at the bar open if a bar opens above TP). If stop and TP are touched in the same bar, the stop counts first. TIME EXIT: exit signal at the close of bar 32 of N (07:45, 15:45 or 23:45 bar), sold at the open of the first bar of the next session (08:00, 16:00 or 00:00): at most 7 hours in the trade. Each spike gives at most one trade; no cooldown beyond that; single cell, no grid. Spot, long only, one position per pair, same numbers on all pairs, one grid cell. Warm-up: 500 bars (direct lookback 21 bars; the recursive SAR resets at each reversal, 500 bars hold many, so runs converge; startup_candle_count = 500). PSAR, Wilder, causal loop, AF start 0.02, step 0.02, max 0.20: bar 0: dir=up, SAR=low(0), EP=high(0), AF=0.02, NXT(0)=SAR(0). For t>=1, S=NXT(t-1): if dir up and low(t)<S, flip down: SAR(t)=EP, EP=low(t), AF=0.02; if dir down and high(t)>S, flip up: SAR(t)=EP, EP=high(t), AF=0.02; else SAR(t)=S and, if high(t)>EP (up) or low(t)<EP (down), EP=that extreme and AF=min(AF+0.02,0.20). Then NXT(t)=SAR(t)+AF*(EP-SAR(t)), capped at min(low(t),low(t-1)) if up, floored at max(high(t),high(t-1)) if down. dir(t)=direction after bar t; NXT(t)=SAR for bar t+1, known at the close of t. Vmean(t)=mean volume of bars t-20..t-1. ENTRY at the close of t if ALL hold: (1) dir(t-1)=down and dir(t)=up; (2) activity filter (b), fixed: volume(t)>=1.5*Vmean(t); (3) close(t)>open(t); (4) pair flat. FILL CHECK (confirm_trade_entry, rate E): skip if NXT(t)<0.97*E. INITIAL STOP, frozen at the fill: S0=min(NXT(t),0.99*E) (1-3% below E); G=NXT(t)-S0 (>=0; 0 when the SAR is already >=1% below E). TRAIL: for each bar k>=t+1 in the trade, stop level L(k)=max(S0, NXT(k-1)-G), from values known at the close of k-1 (L(t+1)=S0, then L follows the rising, accelerating SAR shifted down by the fixed G); sold at L(k) intrabar as soon as low(k) touches it (at open(k) if it opens below). Warm-up: 3000 bars (longest lookback: 20 earlier weekday release bars span about 28 calendar days = 2688 15m bars; 3000 leaves room for missing days; no recursive indicators). CLOCK: convert each bar's UTC open time to America/New_York local time with DST (zoneinfo; 08:30 NY = 12:30 UTC in summer time, 13:30 UTC in winter time). Monday-Friday NY dates only. RELEASE BAR E = the 15m bar whose NY open time is 08:30 on a weekday; range(E) = high(E) - low(E). BASELINE(E) = median of (high - low) of the 08:30 NY bars of the 20 most recent EARLIER weekdays present in the data within the 2900 bars before E (E itself excluded); if fewer than 20 such bars exist, that day has no event. EVENT if range(E) >= 3.0 x BASELINE(E) AND range(E) / open(E) >= 0.008 (the single activity filter, volatility expansion, fixed in advance; no other filter). SHAKEOUT: no action on E itself. ENTRY WINDOW: the 8 bars after E, NY open times 08:45 through 10:30 of the same day. Entry signal at the close of the FIRST bar t in that window with close(t) > high(E); only the first such bar counts (if the pair is not flat then, no trade that day); at most one trade per pair per NY day. Buy at open(t+1) = P. Store low(E) in the enter_tag. STOP: stop price S = min(low(E), 0.99 x P), i.e. d = max(1 - low(E)/P, 0.01), computed at the fill and frozen; a fixed percentage d below P, triggered intrabar as soon as the low touches S (custom_stoploss returning d; native stoploss -0.20 only as engine backstop). TARGET: T = P + 1.5 x (P - S) = P x (1 + 1.5 x d), sold at T as soon as a bar's high reaches it (at the open if a bar opens above T). If stop and target are touched in the same bar, the stop counts. TIME EXIT: the fill bar e is bar 1; exit signal at the close of bar e+31 (the 32nd 15m bar of the trade), sold at the open of e+32, i.e. 8 hours after the fill. First of stop, target, time wins. Spot, long only, one position, whole allowed balance. SESSIONS: fixed 8h blocks starting 00:00, 08:00 and 16:00 UTC; each session has 32 15m bars (offsets 0..31, offset k opens at session start + 15*k minutes). Session candle of a completed session: open = open of its first 15m bar, high = max high, low = min low, close = close of its last 15m bar (built from the 15m bars present in the block). NOISE of a completed session candle: if close > open, NOISE = open - low; if close < open, NOISE = high - open; if close = open, NOISE = min(open - low, high - open). AvgNoise = simple mean of NOISE over the last 10 completed session candles (the current session excluded). GSV = 2 x AvgNoise, fixed for the whole session at its start. O = open of the session's first 15m bar. LEVEL_UP = O + GSV; LEVEL_DN = O - GSV. ARMED: the session is tradable only if GSV / O >= 0.008. FILTER (higher-timeframe direction, the only filter): the close of the last COMPLETED 4h candle (4h candles start 00:00, 04:00, ... UTC, merged with merge_informative_pair so only closed 4h bars are visible at the signal bar's close) > SMA(50) of 4h closes. ENTRY: on a 15m bar of an armed session with offset 0..27 (its close is at least 1h before the session end), signal when ALL hold: bar close > LEVEL_UP; FILTER true at that bar; no open position; no entry already made in this session. Buy at the next 15m bar's open. At most one entry per session; no re-entry in the same session after any exit. EXIT, whichever comes first: (a) GSV exit: a 15m bar after entry closes < LEVEL_DN of the entry session -> sell at the next bar's open; (b) time exit: the session's last 15m bar (offset 31, opening at session start + 7h45m) gives the exit signal at its close, so the position is sold at the next bar's open, which is the session boundary (max hold under 8h); (c) native fixed stop-loss 3% below the entry price (intrabar), the engine-required fixed protection against moves between closed-bar checks (the GSV exit level sits about 2 x GSV >= 1.6% below entry). No take-profit, no trailing stop, no cooldown beyond the once-per-session rule, no pyramiding, no other filters. Spot, long only, one position, whole allowed balance, no pyramiding, no take-profit. DAYS: UTC day = the 24 1h bars opening 00:00..23:00. Daily candle of a completed day: high = max of its 24 highs, low = min of its 24 lows, close = close of its 23:00 bar. For day D: O = open of the 00:00 bar of D. From the 4 completed days D-4, D-3, D-2, D-1: HH = highest daily high, LL = lowest daily low, HC = highest daily close, LC = lowest daily close. RANGE = max(HH - LC, HC - LL). UPPER = O + 0.5 * RANGE; LOWER = O - 0.5 * RANGE. If any of those 4 days or the 00:00 bar of D is missing, no entry on day D. ARMED (day D) only if 0.5 * RANGE / O >= 0.01. FILTER (higher-timeframe direction): 4h candles are UTC blocks starting 00,04,08,12,16,20 built from 1h bars (close = close of the block's last 1h bar); use the most recent 4h bar fully completed at or before the close of the signal bar (as merge_informative_pair provides; the block 00-04 becomes usable at the close of the 03:00 bar); its close must be > SMA(50) of 4h closes (the last 50 completed 4h bars, including that one). ENTRY: at the close of a 1h bar of day D whose open time is between 00:00 and 17:00 inclusive, when ALL hold: close > UPPER; ARMED; FILTER true; no open position; no entry already made on day D. Buy at the next bar open. At most one entry per UTC day; no re-entry on the same day after any exit. EXIT, whichever comes first: (a) a 1h bar closes < LOWER of the entry day D; sell at the next bar open; (b) day end: the close of the 23:00 bar of day D is a sell signal, filled at the open of the 00:00 bar of D+1; (c) time exit: sell at the open of the 12th bar after the entry bar (maximum hold 12 hours); (d) native fixed stop-loss 5% below the entry price, intrabar, as the engine-required gap protection between the closed-bar checks. No cooldown beyond the one-entry-per-day rule. No other filters. ## Rules

- Add a row before running a new hypothesis.
- Do not edit a rule after observing its result; create a new ID.
- Record rejected and negative hypotheses.
- Count parameter variants and cost scenarios as tests.
- The final test period stays locked until the protocol explicitly permits one read.
